## Purpose

This compact index gives agents deterministic aliases for QML components, symbols, dynamic constructs, and local component relationships.

## Scope

- QML roots: `ea_node_editor/ui_qml`
- Extracts imports, root component, ids, object names, properties, signals, functions, handlers, Connections targets, property bindings, instantiated components, local component refs, and dynamic model/delegate/loader constructs.
- Excludes cache, build, dependency, worktree mirror, and generated Excalidraw bundle paths.
